    "identity.counselorName":
      cleanText(identity.counselorName) || cleanText(identity.counselor?.name),
    "identity.principalName":
      cleanText(identity.principalName) ||
      cleanText(identity.schoolLeaderName) ||
      cleanText(identity.school?.principalName),
    "identity.activityLeaderName": cleanText(identity.activityLeaderName),

    "identity.principalSignatureUrl": cleanText(identity.principalSignatureUrl),
    "identity.activityLeaderSignatureUrl": cleanText(identity.activityLeaderSignatureUrl),
    "identity.counselorSignatureUrl": cleanText(identity.counselorSignatureUrl),

    schoolName: cleanText(identity.schoolName || identity.school?.name),
    ministryName: cleanText(identity.ministryName) || "وزارة التعليم",
    counselorName:
      cleanText(identity.counselorName) || cleanText(identity.counselor?.name),
    principalName:
      cleanText(identity.principalName) ||
      cleanText(identity.schoolLeaderName) ||
      cleanText(identity.school?.principalName),

    "report.logoUrl":
      cleanText(identity.logoUrl) ||
      cleanText(identity.schoolLogoUrl) ||
      "/uploads/school-logos/MOE.png",
    "report.platformName": "منصة التوجيه الطلابي",
    "evidence.count": String(previewCaseData.evidences.length),
    evidenceCount: String(previewCaseData.evidences.length),
  };

  previewCaseData.values.forEach((item) => {
    if (item.fieldKey) {
      context[item.fieldKey] = item.value;
      context[`field.${item.fieldKey}`] = item.value;
    }

    if (item.fieldLabel) {
      context[item.fieldLabel] = item.value;
      context[`field.${item.fieldLabel}`] = item.value;
    }
  });

  return context;
}

function buildSnapshotHtml(source: HTMLElement | null) {
  if (!source) return "";

  const clone = source.cloneNode(true) as HTMLElement;
  clone.removeAttribute("aria-hidden");
  clone.removeAttribute("style");
  clone.className = "report-two-a4-host report-two-snapshot-approved-root";

  return `
    <style>
      .report-two-snapshot-approved-root {
        background: #ffffff;
        direction: rtl;
      }

      .report-two-snapshot-approved-root .pdf-report-page,
      .report-two-snapshot-approved-root [data-report-design-page],
      .report-two-snapshot-approved-root .report-design-page {
        width: 210mm !important;
        min-width: 210mm !important;
        max-width: 210mm !important;
        height: 297mm !important;
        min-height: 297mm !important;
        max-height: 297mm !important;
        margin: 0 auto 16px auto !important;
        overflow: hidden !important;
        background: #ffffff !important;
        box-shadow: none !important;
      }

      @media print {
        .report-two-snapshot-approved-root .pdf-report-page,
        .report-two-snapshot-approved-root [data-report-design-page],
        .report-two-snapshot-approved-root .report-design-page {
          margin: 0 !important;
          break-after: page;
          page-break-after: always;
        }

        .report-two-snapshot-approved-root .pdf-report-page:last-child,
        .report-two-snapshot-approved-root [data-report-design-page]:last-child,
        .report-two-snapshot-approved-root .report-design-page:last-child {
          break-after: auto;
          page-break-after: auto;
        }
      }
    </style>
    ${clone.outerHTML}
  `;
}

function buildAdvancedHref({
  advancedEditHref,
  selectedVariantId,
  selectedTemplate,
}: {
  advancedEditHref?: string;
  selectedVariantId: string;
  selectedTemplate?: ReportPrepareTemplateOption | null;
}) {
  if (!advancedEditHref) return "";

  const separator = advancedEditHref.includes("?") ? "&" : "?";
  const params = new URLSearchParams();

  if (selectedVariantId) params.set("variant", selectedVariantId);
  if (selectedTemplate?.id) params.set("templateId", selectedTemplate.id);

  const query = params.toString();

  return query ? `${advancedEditHref}${separator}${query}` : advancedEditHref;
}

export function ReportPrepareA4PreviewModal({
  open,
  caseId,
  payload,
  selectedVariantId,
  selectedTemplate = null,
  preparation,
  advancedEditHref,
  onClose,
}: ReportPrepareA4PreviewModalProps) {
  const shellRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const stackRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const [documentScale, setDocumentScale] = useState(1);
  const [approving, setApproving] = useState(false);
  const [approvedPreviewUrl, setApprovedPreviewUrl] = useState("");
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState("");

  const preparedPayload = useMemo(
    () => (preparation ? applyReportFlowPreparationToPayload(payload, preparation) : null),
    [payload, preparation],
  );

  const template = useMemo(
    () => (preparedPayload ? hydrateTemplate(preparedPayload, selectedTemplate) : null),
    [preparedPayload, selectedTemplate],
  );

  const pages = Array.isArray((template as any)?.pages) ? (template as any).pages : [];
  const activePage = pages[0] || null;
  const activePageId = activePage?.id || "";
  const designId = cleanText((template as any)?.designTemplateId) || "ministry-form";

  const previewCaseData = useMemo(() => {
    if (!preparedPayload || !preparation) return null;

    return buildPreviewCaseData({
      caseId,
      preparedPayload,
      preparation,
      selectedVariantId,
    });
  }, [caseId, preparation, preparedPayload, selectedVariantId]);

  const runtimeContext = useMemo(() => {
    if (!preparedPayload || !previewCaseData) return {};

    return buildRuntimeContext(preparedPayload, previewCaseData);
  }, [preparedPayload, previewCaseData]);

  const reportTitle =
    previewCaseData?.title ||
    preparedPayload?.title ||
    payload.caseInfo.title ||
    "التقرير";

  const advancedHref = buildAdvancedHref({
    advancedEditHref,
    selectedVariantId,
    selectedTemplate,
  });

  const updateScale = useCallback(() => {
    const shell = shellRef.current;

    if (!shell) return;

    const availableWidth = Math.max(320, shell.clientWidth - 28);
    const availableHeight = Math.max(420, shell.clientHeight - 28);
    const nextScale = Math.min(
      1,
      availableWidth / A4_WIDTH_PX,
      availableHeight / A4_HEIGHT_PX,
    );

    setDocumentScale(Number(nextScale.toFixed(4)));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;

    updateScale();

    const shell = shellRef.current;
    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(updateScale);

    if (shell) resizeObserver.observe(shell);

    window.addEventListener("resize", updateScale);

    const fontsReady = (document as any).fonts?.ready;
    if (fontsReady) {
      fontsReady.then(updateScale).catch(() => undefined);
    }

    const timer = window.setTimeout(updateScale, 600);

    return () => {
      resizeObserver.disconnect();
      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateScale);
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
    };
  }, [open, template, previewCaseData, updateScale]);

  async function approveReport() {
    if (approving || approvedPreviewUrl || !preparedPayload || !template || !previewCaseData) {
      return;
    }

    const snapshotHtml = buildSnapshotHtml(stackRef.current);

    if (!snapshotHtml.trim()) {
      setFeedback("تعذر التقاط التقرير للاعتماد.");
      return;
    }

    setApproving(true);
    setFeedback("");

    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        `/api/dashboard/report-2/cases/${encodeURIComponent(caseId)}/approve`,
        {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            reportTitle,
            templateId: selectedTemplate?.id || null,
            templateName: selectedTemplate?.name || cleanText((template as any).name) || null,
            variantId: selectedVariantId || null,
            snapshotPayload: preparedPayload,
            snapshotTemplateJson: template,
            snapshotPagesJson: pages,
            snapshotHtml,
          }),
        },
      );

      const text = await response.text();
      let result: any = {};

      try {
        result = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
      } catch {
        result = { error: text || `HTTP ${response.status}` };
      }

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(result?.details || result?.error || "تعذر اعتماد التقرير.");
      }

      const previewUrl =
        result.previewUrl ||
        `/dashboard/report-2/snapshots/${result.snapshot?.id}/preview`;

      setApprovedPreviewUrl(previewUrl);
      setFeedback("تم اعتماد التقرير وحفظه في الأرشيف.");
    } catch (error) {
      setFeedback(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "تعذر اعتماد التقرير. حاول مرة أخرى.",
      );
    } finally {
      setApproving(false);
    }
  }

  if (!open || typeof document === "undefined") return null;

  return createPortal(
    <div
      dir="rtl"
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[9999] flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden bg-slate-950/55 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm"
    >
      <style>{`
        .prepare-report-two-a4-host {
          overflow: hidden;
        }

        .prepare-report-two-a4-host .pdf-report-page,
        .prepare-report-two-a4-host [data-report-design-page],
        .prepare-report-two-a4-host .report-design-page {
          width: 210mm !important;
          min-width: 210mm !important;
          max-width: 210mm !important;
          height: 297mm !important;
          min-height: 297mm !important;
          max-height: 297mm !important;
          margin: 0 auto !important;
          overflow: hidden !important;
          box-sizing: border-box !important;
          border: 1.5px solid #64748b !important;
          outline: 6px solid rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.05) !important;
          box-shadow: 0 20px 50px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.16) !important;
          background: #ffffff !important;
          aspect-ratio: 210 / 297 !important;
        }

        .prepare-report-two-a4-host .pdf-report-page::before,
        .prepare-report-two-a4-host .pdf-report-page::after {
          display: none !important;
        }

        .prepare-report-two-a4-host [class~="sm:grid-cols-2"],
        .prepare-report-two-a4-host [class~="md:grid-cols-2"],
        .prepare-report-two-a4-host [class~="lg:grid-cols-2"] {
          grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)) !important;
        }

        .prepare-report-two-a4-host [class~="sm:grid-cols-3"],
        .prepare-report-two-a4-host [class~="md:grid-cols-3"],
        .prepare-report-two-a4-host [class~="lg:grid-cols-3"] {
          grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)) !important;
        }

        .prepare-report-two-a4-host [class~="sm:grid-cols-4"],
        .prepare-report-two-a4-host [class~="md:grid-cols-4"],
        .prepare-report-two-a4-host [class~="lg:grid-cols-4"] {
          grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr)) !important;
        }
      `}</style>

      <section
        className="relative flex h-[92vh] w-[min(94vw,620px)] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-[2.3rem] bg-white p-3 shadow-2xl shadow-slate-950/25 ring-1 ring-white/80"
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="absolute inset-x-4 top-4 z-20 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="inline-flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-[1.2rem] bg-white text-slate-950 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-slate-200 transition hover:bg-slate-50"
            aria-label="إغلاق"
          >
            <X className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {advancedHref ? (
              <Link
                href={advancedHref}
                className="inline-flex h-11 items-center justify-center rounded-[1.2rem] bg-white px-4 text-xs font-black text-slate-950 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-slate-200 transition hover:bg-slate-50"
              >
                تعديل متقدم
              </Link>
            ) : null}

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={approveReport}
              disabled={approving || Boolean(approvedPreviewUrl)}
              className="inline-flex h-11 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-[1.2rem] bg-emerald-700 px-4 text-xs font-black text-white shadow-sm transition hover:bg-emerald-800 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
            >
              {approving ? (
                <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
              ) : (
                <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4" />
              )}
              {approvedPreviewUrl ? "تم الاعتماد" : approving ? "جارٍ الاعتماد..." : "اعتماد التقرير"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {feedback ? (
          <div className="absolute inset-x-4 top-17 z-20 flex justify-center">
            <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white px-4 py-2 text-xs font-black text-slate-800 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-slate-100">
              {feedback}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null}

        <div
          ref={shellRef}
          className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-[2rem] bg-gradient-to-b from-sky-50 via-white to-sky-50 p-3 pt-16 ring-1 ring-sky-100"
        >
          {preparation && template && previewCaseData && preparedPayload && activePage ? (
            <div
              className="relative mx-auto"
              style={{
                width: `${A4_WIDTH_PX * documentScale}px`,
                height: `${A4_HEIGHT_PX * documentScale}px`,
              }}
            >
              <div
                className="prepare-report-two-a4-host absolute left-1/2 top-0"
                style={{
                  width: "210mm",
                  minWidth: "210mm",
                  maxWidth: "210mm",
                  transform: `translateX(-50%) scale(${documentScale})`,
                  transformOrigin: "top center",
                }}
              >
                <ReportDesignRenderer
                  suppressAutoEvidencePages
                  chromeLayout="split"
                  designId={designId as ReportDesignId}
                  template={template as any}
                  activePage={activePage as any}
                  activePageId={activePageId}
                  context={runtimeContext}
                  previewCase={previewCaseData}
                  onActivePageChange={() => undefined}
                  onAddPage={() => undefined}
                  onMovePage={() => undefined}
                  onDeletePage={() => undefined}
                  canMovePage={() => false}
                  canDeletePage={() => false}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <section className="max-w-sm rounded-[2rem] bg-white p-6 text-center shadow-sm ring-1 ring-amber-100">
              <p className="text-lg font-black text-slate-950">المعاينة غير جاهزة</p>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm font-bold leading-7 text-slate-500">
                ارجع للتحضير ثم احفظ وصف التنفيذ والحقول قبل المعاينة.
              </p>
            </section>
          )}
        </div>

        {approvedPreviewUrl ? (
          <Link
            href={approvedPreviewUrl}
            className="mt-3 inline-flex h-11 items-center justify-center rounded-[1.2rem] bg-sky-600 text-sm font-black text-white shadow-sm transition hover:bg-sky-700"
          >
            فتح التقرير المعتمد
          </Link>
        ) : null}

        {template && activePage ? (
          <section
            ref={stackRef}
            aria-hidden="true"
            data-report-two-snapshot-source="prepare-print-stack"
            className="prepare-report-two-a4-host report-two-a4-host"
            style={{
              position: "fixed",
              top: 0,
              left: "-10000px",
              width: "210mm",
              minHeight: "297mm",
              background: "#ffffff",
              pointerEvents: "none",
              zIndex: -1,
            }}
          >
            <ReportDesignRenderer
              suppressAutoEvidencePages
              renderMode="stack"
              chromeLayout="split"
              designId={designId as ReportDesignId}
              template={template as any}
              activePage={activePage as any}
              activePageId={activePageId}
              context={runtimeContext}
              previewCase={previewCaseData || {}}
              onActivePageChange={() => undefined}
              onAddPage={() => undefined}
              onMovePage={() => undefined}
              onDeletePage={() => undefined}
              canMovePage={() => false}
              canDeletePage={() => false}
            />
          </section>
        ) : null}
      </section>
    </div>,
    document.body,
  );
}
